    @pytest.mark.parametrize("form,kind", CASES, ids=[f"{f}-{k}" for f, k in CASES])
    def test_a_real_token_is_found_across_every_place_a_read_begins_or_ends(sandbox, project, stand_in, slots, form, kind):
        layout, writer, ext = FORMS[form]
        make, before, after = TOKENS[kind]
        content = support.tagged(layout, support.SWEEP_SIZE)
        clean = f"{R}/sweep/clean{ext}"
        edges = support.learn(sandbox, project, stand_in, clean, lambda path: writer(path, content), content)
        token = make(f"{form}-{kind}").encode()
        places = edges.chosen(len(token) + UNCERTAIN + 2, len(content) - len(token) - UNCERTAIN - 2, CAP)
        assert places, f"the learning run showed no place where a read begins or ends inside {form}"
        cases = []
        for edge in places:
            for at in _offsets(kind, edge, token.decode()):
                data = support.place(content, at, token, before, after)
                if data is not None and _fits(form, at, token, before, after):
                    cases.append((edge, at, data))
        rels = []
        for number, (_edge, _at, data) in enumerate(cases):
            rel = f"{R}/sweep/case-{number:05d}{ext}"
            writer(project / rel, data)
            rels.append(rel)
        with support.slot(slots):
            run = support.run_check(sandbox, project)
        assert run.returncode is not None, f"the check did not end within its declared limit\n{run.describe()}"
        assert token.decode() not in run.stdout + run.stderr, "the check printed the token"
        assert not run.names(clean), f"the clean content of {form} was named as holding a secret\n{run.describe()}"
        missed = {}
        for rel, (edge, at, _data) in zip(rels, cases):
            if not run.names(rel):
                missed.setdefault(edge, []).append(at - edge)
        kinds = {edge: ("a read of gitleaks" if edge in edges.reads else "a split of the check") for edge in places}

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("holds", ("a-token", "no-token"))
    def test_a_store_another_process_holds_locked_is_judged_by_its_raw_bytes(sandbox, project, holds):
        secret = support.ghp_token("locked") if holds == "a-token" else support.marker("lockedclean")
        rel = f"{R}/locked.db"
        _sqlite(project, rel, secret)
        holder = sqlite3.connect(project / rel, isolation_level=None)
        holder.execute("BEGIN EXCLUSIVE")
        try:
            probe = sqlite3.connect(f"file:{project / rel}?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=0.2)
            with pytest.raises(sqlite3.OperationalError):
                probe.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM notes").fetchone()
            probe.close()
            run = support.run_check(sandbox, project)
        finally:
            holder.execute("ROLLBACK")
            holder.close()
        assert run.returncode is not None, f"the check did not end within its declared limit\n{run.describe()}"
        if holds == "a-token":

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("form,kind", CASES, ids=[f"{f}-{k}" for f, k in CASES])
    def test_a_real_token_is_found_across_every_place_a_read_begins_or_ends(sandbox, project, stand_in, slots, form, kind):
        layout, writer, ext = FORMS[form]
        make, before, after = TOKENS[kind]
        content = support.tagged(layout, support.SWEEP_SIZE)
        clean = f"{R}/sweep/clean{ext}"
        edges = support.learn(sandbox, project, stand_in, clean, lambda path: writer(path, content), content)
        token = make(f"{form}-{kind}").encode()
        places = edges.chosen(len(token) + UNCERTAIN + 2, len(content) - len(token) - UNCERTAIN - 2, CAP)
        assert places, f"the learning run showed no place where a read begins or ends inside {form}"
        cases = []
        for edge in places:
            for at in _offsets(kind, edge, token.decode()):
                data = support.place(content, at, token, before, after)
                if data is not None and _fits(form, at, token, before, after):
                    cases.append((edge, at, data))
        rels = []
        for number, (_edge, _at, data) in enumerate(cases):
            rel = f"{R}/sweep/case-{number:05d}{ext}"
            writer(project / rel, data)
            rels.append(rel)
        with support.slot(slots):
            run = support.run_check(sandbox, project)
        assert run.returncode is not None, f"the check did not end within its declared limit\n{run.describe()}"
        assert token.decode() not in run.stdout + run.stderr, "the check printed the token"
        assert not run.names(clean), f"the clean content of {form} was named as holding a secret\n{run.describe()}"
        missed = {}
        for rel, (edge, at, _data) in zip(rels, cases):
            if not run.names(rel):
                missed.setdefault(edge, []).append(at - edge)
        kinds = {edge: ("a read of gitleaks" if edge in edges.reads else "a split of the check") for edge in places}

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("holds", ("a-token", "no-token"))
    def test_a_store_another_process_holds_locked_is_judged_by_its_raw_bytes(sandbox, project, holds):
        secret = support.ghp_token("locked") if holds == "a-token" else support.marker("lockedclean")
        rel = f"{R}/locked.db"
        _sqlite(project, rel, secret)
        holder = sqlite3.connect(project / rel, isolation_level=None)
        holder.execute("BEGIN EXCLUSIVE")
        try:
            probe = sqlite3.connect(f"file:{project / rel}?mode=ro", uri=True, timeout=0.2)
            with pytest.raises(sqlite3.OperationalError):
                probe.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM notes").fetchone()
            probe.close()
            run = support.run_check(sandbox, project)
        finally:
            holder.execute("ROLLBACK")
            holder.close()
        assert run.returncode is not None, f"the check did not end within its declared limit\n{run.describe()}"
        if holds == "a-token":
            assert secret not in run.stdout + run.stderr, "the check printed the secret"
            assert run.returncode != 0 and run.names(rel), (
                f"a store held locked by another process holds a token in its bytes; the check must judge it by its raw "
                f"bytes and name it (exit code {run.returncode})\n{run.describe()}")

    def test_a_real_token_is_found_across_every_place_a_read_begins_or_ends(sandbox, project, stand_in, slots, form, kind):
        layout, writer, ext = FORMS[form]
        make, before, after = TOKENS[kind]
        content = support.tagged(layout, support.SWEEP_SIZE)
        clean = f"{R}/sweep/clean{ext}"
        edges = support.learn(sandbox, project, stand_in, clean, lambda path: writer(path, content), content)
        token = make(f"{form}-{kind}").encode()
        places = edges.chosen(len(token) + UNCERTAIN + 2, len(content) - len(token) - UNCERTAIN - 2, CAP)
        assert places, f"the learning run showed no place where a read begins or ends inside {form}"
        cases = []
        for edge in places:

                data = support.place(content, at, token, before, after)
                if data is not None and _fits(form, at, token, before, after):
                    cases.append((edge, at, data))
        rels = []

            rel = f"{R}/sweep/case-{number:05d}{ext}"
            writer(project / rel, data)
            rels.append(rel)
        with support.slot(slots):
            run = support.run_check(sandbox, project)
        assert run.returncode is not None, f"the check did not end within its declared limit\n{run.describe()}"
        assert token.decode() not in run.stdout + run.stderr, "the check printed the token"
        assert not run.names(clean), f"the clean content of {form} was named as holding a secret\n{run.describe()}"
        missed = {}
        for rel, (edge, at, _data) in zip(rels, cases):
            if not run.names(rel):
                missed.setdefault(edge, []).append(at - edge)
        kinds = {edge: ("a read of gitleaks" if edge in edges.reads else "a split of the check") for edge in places}

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("form,kind", CASES, ids=[f"{f}-{k}" for f, k in CASES])
    def test_a_real_token_is_found_across_every_place_a_read_begins_or_ends(sandbox, project, stand_in, slots, form, kind):
        layout, writer, ext = FORMS[form]
        make, before, after = TOKENS[kind]
        content = support.tagged(layout, support.SWEEP_SIZE)
        clean = f"{R}/sweep/clean{ext}"
        edges = support.learn(sandbox, project, stand_in, clean, lambda path: writer(path, content), content)
        token = make(f"{form}-{kind}").encode()
        places = edges.chosen(len(token) + UNCERTAIN + 2, len(content) - len(token) - UNCERTAIN - 2, CAP)
        assert places, f"the learning run showed no place where a read begins or ends inside {form}"
        cases = []
        for edge in places:
            for at in _offsets(kind, edge, token.decode()):
                data = support.place(content, at, token, before, after)
                if data is not None and _fits(form, at, token, before, after):
                    cases.append((edge, at, data))
        rels = []
        for number, (_edge, _at, data) in enumerate(cases):
            rel = f"{R}/sweep/case-{number:05d}{ext}"
            writer(project / rel, data)
            rels.append(rel)
        with support.slot(slots):
            run = support.run_check(sandbox, project)
        assert run.returncode is not None, f"the check did not end within its declared limit\n{run.describe()}"
        assert token.decode() not in run.stdout + run.stderr, "the check printed the token"
        assert not run.names(clean), f"the clean content of {form} was named as holding a secret\n{run.describe()}"
        missed = {}
        for rel, (edge, at, _data) in zip(rels, cases):
            if not run.names(rel):
                missed.setdefault(edge, []).append(at - edge)
        kinds = {edge: ("a read of gitleaks" if edge in edges.reads else "a split of the check") for edge in places}
>       assert not missed, (
            f"{form}, {kind}: the check missed {sum(map(len, missed.values()))} of {len(cases)} real tokens at a word "
            f"boundary across the places where a read begins or ends:\n" + "\n".join(
                f"  content offset {edge} ({kinds[edge]}): token start at {sorted(offs)} bytes from it"
                for edge, offs in sorted(missed.items())) + f"\n{run.describe(600)}")
E       AssertionError: plain-oneline, github-pat: the check missed 78 of 141 real tokens at a word boundary across the places where a read begins or ends:

    def test_bytes_of_different_values_never_join_into_a_finding(sandbox, project, how):

_ test_a_word_internal_string_of_every_default_rule_is_no_finding_at_a_read_edge[plain-lines-a-read-begins-inside-the-word] _
[gw5] linux -- Python 3.12.3 /usr/bin/python3

sandbox = Sandbox(base=PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw5/test_a_word_internal_string_of7/w2-6...e=PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw5/test_a_word_internal_string_of7/w2-69/pycache'))
project = PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw5/test_a_word_internal_string_of7/w2-69/project')
stand_in = StandIn(bin=PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw5/test_a_word_internal_string_of7/w2-69...lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw5/test_a_word_internal_string_of7/w2-69/elsewhere/gitleaks-stand-in/kept'))
slots = PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/w2-69-slots')
form = 'plain-lines', side = 'a-read-begins-inside-the-word'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("side", ("a-read-begins-inside-the-word", "a-read-ends-inside-the-word"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("form", ("plain-lines", "sqlite-long-value"))
    def test_a_word_internal_string_of_every_default_rule_is_no_finding_at_a_read_edge(sandbox, project, stand_in, slots,
                                                                                        form, side):
        ext, writer = _default_writer(form)
        content = support.tagged("lines", DEFAULT_SIZE)
        clean = f"{R}/edges/clean{ext}"
        edges = support.learn(sandbox, project, stand_in, clean, lambda path: writer(path, content), content)
        places = edges.chosen(400, len(content) - 900, DEFAULT_CAP)
        assert places, f"the learning run showed no place where a read begins inside {form}"
        rels, strings, empty = [], [], []
        for rule in DEFAULT_RULES:
            if rule in NO_CASE:
                continue
            built = support.rule_string(rule)
            if side == "a-read-ends-inside-the-word" and not built.suffix:
                empty.append(rule)
                continue
            data, taken = bytearray(content), []
            for edge in places:
                if side == "a-read-begins-inside-the-word":     # the token-shaped part begins where the read begins
                    text, at = (built.prefix + built.token + built.tail).encode(), edge - len(built.prefix)
                else:                                           # the token-shaped part ends where the read ends
                    text, at = (built.token + built.suffix + built.tail).encode(), edge - len(built.token)
                start, end = at - 1, at + len(text) + 1
                if any(start < b and a < end for a, b in taken):
                    continue
                placed = support.place(data, at, text)
                if placed is not None:
                    data, taken = placed, taken + [(start, end)]
            if not taken:
                continue
            rel = f"{R}/edges/{rule}{ext}"
            writer(project / rel, data)
            rels.append(rel)
            strings.append(built.token)
        assert len(rels) >= 40, f"too few default rules placed: {len(rels)}"
        with support.slot(slots):
            run = support.run_check(sandbox, project)
        assert not any(token in run.stdout + run.stderr for token in strings), "the check printed a token-shaped string"
>       _assert_none_named(run, rels, f"{form}, the default rules, {side} (rules without such a case: {empty})")

sandbox = Sandbox(base=PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw1/test_a_word_internal_string_of8/w2-6...e=PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw1/test_a_word_internal_string_of8/w2-69/pycache'))
project = PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw1/test_a_word_internal_string_of8/w2-69/project')
stand_in = StandIn(bin=PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw1/test_a_word_internal_string_of8/w2-69...lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/popen-gw1/test_a_word_internal_string_of8/w2-69/elsewhere/gitleaks-stand-in/kept'))
slots = PosixPath('/tmp/gov-os-lead-W2-69/pytest-of-usain/pytest-0/w2-69-slots')
form = 'sqlite-long-value', side = 'a-read-ends-inside-the-word'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("side", ("a-read-begins-inside-the-word", "a-read-ends-inside-the-word"))
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("form", ("plain-lines", "sqlite-long-value"))
    def test_a_word_internal_string_of_every_default_rule_is_no_finding_at_a_read_edge(sandbox, project, stand_in, slots,
                                                                                        form, side):
        ext, writer = _default_writer(form)
        content = support.tagged("lines", DEFAULT_SIZE)
        clean = f"{R}/edges/clean{ext}"
        edges = support.learn(sandbox, project, stand_in, clean, lambda path: writer(path, content), content)
        places = edges.chosen(400, len(content) - 900, DEFAULT_CAP)
        assert places, f"the learning run showed no place where a read begins inside {form}"
        rels, strings, empty = [], [], []
        for rule in DEFAULT_RULES:
            if rule in NO_CASE:
                continue
            built = support.rule_string(rule)
            if side == "a-read-ends-inside-the-word" and not built.suffix:
                empty.append(rule)
                continue
            data, taken = bytearray(content), []
            for edge in places:
                if side == "a-read-begins-inside-the-word":     # the token-shaped part begins where the read begins
                    text, at = (built.prefix + built.token + built.tail).encode(), edge - len(built.prefix)
                else:                                           # the token-shaped part ends where the read ends
                    text, at = (built.token + built.suffix + built.tail).encode(), edge - len(built.token)
                start, end = at - 1, at + len(text) + 1
                if any(start < b and a < end for a, b in taken):
                    continue
                placed = support.place(data, at, text)
                if placed is not None:
                    data, taken = placed, taken + [(start, end)]
            if not taken:
                continue
            rel = f"{R}/edges/{rule}{ext}"
            writer(project / rel, data)
            rels.append(rel)
            strings.append(built.token)
        assert len(rels) >= 40, f"too few default rules placed: {len(rels)}"
        with support.slot(slots):
            run = support.run_check(sandbox, project)
        assert not any(token in run.stdout + run.stderr for token in strings), "the check printed a token-shaped string"
>       _assert_none_named(run, rels, f"{form}, the default rules, {side} (rules without such a case: {empty})")
